| 2026-11-11 | #755 S4 r3 | R21 | Damage-cover allocation lifecycle (N8, amends R18): persist the allocation or derive it? | **Derive, do not persist** (DECISION RULE: one source of truth - a persisted allocation table would itself need reconciling on every dismiss, reject and reversal). One helper under the client row lock: each DAMAGED write-off line's strict FIFO share plus the drift of the frozen live claims (share - offered) is offered to the lines with no claim yet, in stable order, clamped to [0, line]. It runs on every write-off offer (auto + manual; sweeps any unclaimed line the group now owes - its own event then skips it on the line key), on every damage-claim offer (a late / replayed / back-dated / equal-instant cover is offered only what the write-off claims since it have not already claimed; equal instant counts as before), and again when a damage claim is DISMISSED, REJECTED or source-reversed, or a claim in the group is released by a reversal. Invariant: the group's live claims never exceed its damaged loss. Residuals: a claim keeps its frozen quantity (V531173), so drift is settled on the next unclaimed line of the same item and lot; a damage claim offered 0 is not re-offered automatically when the write-off claims are later withdrawn - a manual offer on the in-leg recovers it. No migration (V531194 not used). | none | #755 R42 S4 r3 N8 |

ONE live-claim rule for every allocation and cover read (`Wh3plCustodyClaimVocabulary.isLiveClaim` / `liveClaimSql`): not DISMISSED/REJECTED; a source-reversed claim stays live while payable (RAISED..SETTLED); a reversed WITHIN_ALLOWANCE (allowance released) is not live. A reversed line stays in the window while a claim on it is live. Unabsorbed negative drift flags each over-claimed line's primary claim (`over_claim_quantity`, newest first, cleared when it no longer exceeds its share) - same pattern as the reversal stamp; Survey/Accept/Settle are not refused. Release derives its groups and clients from the reversed movement's own lines, not only its claims | none (V531194: flag + top-up columns) | #755 R42 S4 r4 N8 |
